# Onlymonster API

#### Base URLs

* **Production:** `https://omapi.onlymonster.ai`

#### Rate limits

* **Token limit:** 25 requests per second across all endpoints.
* **Endpoint limit:** 15 requests per second per endpoint (default). Some endpoints override this — see the description on each endpoint.
* **Platform account limit:** 1 request per second per platform account on selected endpoint groups — endpoints that apply it say so in their description.
* Exceeding either limit returns `429 Rate limit exceeded`.<br>

#### API key permissions

Each API key carries the permissions chosen when it was created. A request to an endpoint the key is not permitted to call returns `403` with `code: API_KEY_PERMISSION_DENIED` and the missing permission in `details.required_permission`.

Permissions are independent of the key's account access: a request needs both. Keys created before permissions were introduced hold every permission. Each endpoint's description names its required permission.

* `accounts.view` — `GET /api/v0/accounts`, `GET /api/v0/accounts/{account_id}`
* `dashboard.view` — `GET …/transactions`, `GET …/chargebacks`
* `team_metrics.view` — `GET /api/v0/users/metrics`, `GET /api/v0/members`
* `traffic_metrics.view` — `GET …/tracking-links`, `GET …/tracking-link-users`, `GET …/trial-links`, `GET …/trial-link-users`, `GET …/subscriptions`
* `onlyfans.messages` — `GET /api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/fans`, `GET` / `POST …/chats/{chat_id}/messages`, `PATCH …/fans/{fan_id}`
* `onlyfans.collections` — `POST …/fans/{fan_id}/subscribe`
* `onlyfans.statements` — `GET /proxy/v0/…/statements/balances`, `GET` / `POST /proxy/v0/…/statements/payout-requests`
* `auto_messages.view` — `GET …/auto-message-campaigns`, `GET …/auto-message-campaigns/{campaign_id}`
* `auto_messages.edit` — `POST …/auto-message-campaigns`, `PATCH …/auto-message-campaigns/{campaign_id}/status`, `POST …/auto-message-campaigns/{campaign_id}/unsend`
* `media_uploader.manage` — All `…/vault/…` endpoints: folders, medias, thumbnails and media uploads
* `settings.view` — Account session export (enabled per organisation)

<br>

## Get list of accounts.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 15 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`accounts.view\`

## Get fan IDs for an account based on recent chat activity.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second. Also 1 request per second for each platform account.\
> \
> \*\*Platforms:\*\* OnlyFans and Fansly accounts. An account on a different platform gets \`400\`.\
> \
> Returns the fans that sent or got a message in the last 2 days. Each fan occurs 1 time.\
> \
> \*\*Fansly accounts:\*\* The API reads the Fansly chat list, newest first, and stops at the first chat without a message in that period. A Fansly account gets a maximum of 1000 fan IDs. When Fansly limits the rate of the account, the request gets \`429\` and no part of the list. The list of a Fansly account can be up to 60 seconds old.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.messages\` for an account on \`onlyfans\`\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`fansly.messages\` for an account on \`fansly\`

```json

Also 1 request per second for each platform account.\n\n**Platforms:** OnlyFans and Fansly accounts. Each fan occurs 1 time.\n\n**Fansly accounts:** The API reads the Fansly chat list, newest first, and stops at the first chat without a message in that period. ## Create a payout request for a platform account

> Requests a withdrawal of the given amount from the account's available balance.\
> \
> This action is irreversible: OnlyFans does not support idempotency for this request. Do not blindly retry on timeout — first check the payout requests list to see whether the request was created.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second per platform account, shared across all \`/proxy/\` endpoints.\
> \
> This endpoint is in gradual rollout: it is enabled per organization, and organizations without access receive 403 Forbidden.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.statements\`

```json

## Retrieve Fansly wallets for a platform account

> Returns the account's Fansly wallets. The amount available for payout is the \`balance\` of the earnings wallet (\`type\` 2). The order of the wallets is not fixed: pick a wallet by \`type\`.\
> \
> Fansly does not return a total balance. The total Fansly shows is the \`balance\` of the earnings wallet (\`type\` 2) from \`GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform\_account\_id}/wallets\` plus \`pendingBalance\` from \`GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform\_account\_id}/wallets/earnings\`.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second per platform account, shared across all \`/proxy/\` endpoints.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`fansly.statements\`

```json

The amount available for payout is the `balance` of the earnings wallet (`type` 2). The order of the wallets is not fixed: pick a wallet by `type`.\n\nFansly does not return a total balance. The total Fansly shows is the `balance` of the earnings wallet (`type` 2) from `GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform_account_id}/wallets` plus `pendingBalance` from `GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform_account_id}/wallets/earnings`.\n\n**Rate limit:** 1 request per second per platform account, shared across all `/proxy/` endpoints.\n\n**Required permission:** `fansly.statements`","parameters":[{"schema":{"type":"string","enum":["fansly"]},"in":"path","name":"platform","required":true,"description":"Platform name. The only supported value is `fansly`."},{"schema":{"anyOf":[{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Big integer id as string","type":"string"},{"type":"string"}]},"in":"path","name":"platform_account_id","required":true,"description":"Unique identifier of the Fansly account."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"success":{"description":"Fansly success flag; always `true` in a successful response.","type":"boolean","enum":[true]},"response":{"description":"The account's wallets: the earnings wallet (`type` 2) and the main wallet (`type` 1).","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Unique identifier of the wallet; `-1` when the account has no such wallet yet, and its `balance` is then `0`.","anyOf":[{"type":"string"},{"type":"integer"}]},"balance":{"description":"Wallet balance in thousandths of a US dollar (`95608` = $95.608). On the earnings wallet (`type` 2) this is the amount available for payout, which Fansly displays truncated to cents ($95.60).","type":"integer"},"balance64":{"description":"Same amount as `balance`, expressed as a 64-bit integer. Retry later.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"}},"required":["message"]}}}}}}}}}

## Retrieve the Fansly earnings wallet summary for a platform account

> Returns the pending balance of the Fansly earnings wallet.\
> \
> Fansly does not return a total balance. The total Fansly shows is the \`balance\` of the earnings wallet (\`type\` 2) from \`GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform\_account\_id}/wallets\` plus \`pendingBalance\` from \`GET /proxy/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform\_account\_id}/wallets/earnings\`.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second per platform account, shared across all \`/proxy/\` endpoints.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`fansly.statements\`

## Get chat messages for a specific chat.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second\
> \
> \*\*Platforms:\*\* OnlyFans and Fansly accounts. An account on a different platform gets \`400\`.\
> \
> \*\*Read state:\*\* On OnlyFans, a read marks the chat as read, the same as when the creator opens it there.\
> The unread messages of the chat then go from the unread list of the creator. On Fansly, a read does not mark the chat as read.\
> To watch incoming OnlyFans messages without a change of the read state, subscribe to the \`chat.message\` webhook.\
> \
> \*\*Fansly accounts:\*\*\
> \- \`chat\_id\` is the Fansly user ID of the fan, as a digit string without a leading zero. A different value gets \`400\`.\
> \- The newest messages come first. \`message\_id\` gives the messages that are older than that message. \`order=asc\` gets \`400\`.\
> \- The first read for a fan can create an empty chat (a DM group) with that fan on Fansly. A send to the fan has the same effect.\
> \- The API does not return Fansly media files, prices or purchase data yet. These fields are \`null\`, and \`media\` is empty. \`media\_count\` shows that a message has media.\
> \- \`sender\` and \`created\_by\` are always empty for Fansly.\
> \- Fansly can refuse a chat with a fan. The API then answers \`422\` with \`code: FAN\_CHAT\_NOT\_AVAILABLE\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.messages\` for an account on \`onlyfans\`\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`fansly.messages\` for an account on \`fansly\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"messages","description":"Endpoints for sending messages."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/chats/{chat_id}/messages":{"get":{"summary":"Get chat messages for a specific chat.","tags":["messages"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 1 request per second\n\n**Platforms:** OnlyFans and Fansly accounts. An account on a different platform gets `400`.\n\n**Read state:** On OnlyFans, a read marks the chat as read, the same as when the creator opens it there.\nThe unread messages of the chat then go from the unread list of the creator. On Fansly, a read does not mark the chat as read.\nTo watch incoming OnlyFans messages without a change of the read state, subscribe to the `chat.message` webhook.\n\n**Fansly accounts:**\n- `chat_id` is the Fansly user ID of the fan, as a digit string without a leading zero. A different value gets `400`.\n- The newest messages come first. `message_id` gives the messages that are older than that message. `order=asc` gets `400`.\n- The first read for a fan can create an empty chat (a DM group) with that fan on Fansly. A send to the fan has the same effect.\n- The API does not return Fansly media files, prices or purchase data yet. These fields are `null`, and `media` is empty. `media_count` shows that a message has media.\n- `sender` and `created_by` are always empty for Fansly.\n- Fansly can refuse a chat with a fan. The API then answers `422` with `code: FAN_CHAT_NOT_AVAILABLE`.\n\n**Required permission:** `onlyfans.messages` for an account on `onlyfans`\n\n**Required permission:** `fansly.messages` for an account on `fansly`","parameters":[{"schema":{"minimum":1,"maximum":100,"default":100,"type":"integer"},"in":"query","name":"limit","required":false},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"message_id","required":false,"description":"ID of the last message from the previous page. Used for pagination. `0` means no cursor. Send a Fansly message ID as a digit string. For OnlyFans, the maximum is 2^53 - 1."},{"schema":{"anyOf":[{"type":"string","enum":["asc"]},{"type":"string","enum":["desc"]}]},"in":"query","name":"order","required":false,"description":"`desc` (the default) gives the newest messages first. `asc` is available only for an OnlyFans account. A Fansly account gets `400` for `asc`."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"},{"schema":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"chat_id","required":true}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"items":{"type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"ID of the message. An integer for OnlyFans. A digit string for Fansly, because a Fansly ID can be more than 2^53.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"string"}]},"text":{"description":"Text of the message. HTML for OnlyFans. Plain text for Fansly, or an empty string when the message has no text.","type":"string"},"from_user":{"description":"Platform user ID of the sender. An integer for OnlyFans. A digit string for Fansly, because a Fansly ID can be more than 2^53.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"string"}]},"is_sent_by_me":{"description":"true = sent by account owner (creator), false = sent by fan","type":"boolean"},"created_at":{"type":"string"},"media":{"description":"Media files of the message. Always empty for Fansly, because the API does not return Fansly media files yet.","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"integer"},"type":{"type":"string"},"can_view":{"type":"boolean"},"is_ready":{"type":"boolean"},"has_error":{"anyOf":[{"type":"boolean"},{"type":"null"}]},"converted_to_video":{"type":"boolean"},"created_at":{"anyOf":[{"type":"string"},{"type":"null"}]},"thumbnail_url":{"type":"string"}},"required":["id","type","can_view","is_ready","has_error","converted_to_video","created_at","thumbnail_url"]}},"media_count":{"description":"Number of media files in the message. For Fansly, this number shows that the message has media, but `media` stays empty.","type":"integer"},"is_opened":{"description":"read/unread: false = unread. `null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"is_new":{"description":"`null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"price":{"description":"`null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"number"}]},"is_free":{"description":"`null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"can_purchase":{"description":"`null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"can_purchase_reason":{"description":"`null` for Fansly, because the API does not return this value yet.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]},"sender":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"Who sent the message on the creator side. Retry later.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"}},"required":["message"]}}}}}}}}}
```

## Send a message to a chat.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second\
> \
> The message text is scanned by the content-moderation service before being enqueued.\
> Requests whose text matches restricted words or topics return \`400\` with\
> \`code: "RESTRICTED\_WORDS\_DETECTED"\` and the matching \`words\` / \`topics\` in \`details\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.messages\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"messages","description":"Endpoints for sending messages."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/chats/{chat_id}/messages":{"post":{"summary":"Send a message to a chat.","tags":["messages"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 1 request per second\n\nThe message text is scanned by the content-moderation service before being enqueued.\nRequests whose text matches restricted words or topics return `400` with\n`code: \"RESTRICTED_WORDS_DETECTED\"` and the matching `words` / `topics` in `details`.\n\n**Required permission:** `onlyfans.messages`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"allOf":[{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"text":{"anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"minLength":1,"maxLength":5000,"description":"The message text content. Required unless a media file is present.","type":"string"}]},"price":{"default":null,"description":"Price for the message (0 or null for free, or between 3-200 for paid content). A paid message (price >= 3) must carry at least one paid item.","anyOf":[{"type":"number","enum":[0]},{"minimum":3,"maximum":200,"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"locked_text":{"default":false,"description":"If true, the text is locked behind the paywall (paid text). Requires price >= 3 and counts as a paid item, so media is not required.","type":"boolean"},"media":{"maxItems":100,"default":[],"description":"Media attachments (already uploaded to platform)","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"description":"Existing platform media ID","type":"string"},"type":{"type":"string","enum":["image","video","audio","gif"]},"is_paid":{"description":"If true, this media is locked behind the paywall and excluded from the preview shown to the recipient before purchase. Only meaningful when price >= 3.","type":"boolean"}},"required":["id","type"]}}},"required":["text","price","locked_text","media"]},{"if":{"properties":{"price":{"type":"number","minimum":3}},"required":["price"]},"then":{"anyOf":[{"properties":{"locked_text":{"enum":[true]}},"required":["locked_text"]},{"properties":{"media":{"contains":{"properties":{"is_paid":{"enum":[true]}},"required":["is_paid"]}}},"required":["media"]}]}},{"if":{"properties":{"media":{"contains":{"properties":{"is_paid":{"enum":[true]}},"required":["is_paid"]}}},"required":["media"]},"then":{"properties":{"price":{"type":"number","minimum":3}},"required":["price"]}},{"if":{"properties":{"locked_text":{"enum":[true]}},"required":["locked_text"]},"then":{"properties":{"price":{"type":"number","minimum":3}},"required":["price"]}},{"if":{"properties":{"text":{"enum":[null]}},"required":["text"]},"then":{"properties":{"media":{"minItems":1}},"required":["media"]}}]}}}},"parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"},{"schema":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"chat_id","required":true}],"responses":{"202":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"send_id":{"format":"uuid","description":"Unique identifier for tracking this send by webhook","type":"string"}},"required":["send_id"]}}}},"400":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"type":"object","properties":{"message":{"type":"string"},"code":{"type":"string","enum":["RESTRICTED_WORDS_DETECTED"]},"details":{"type":"object","properties":{"words":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}},"topics":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}},"required":["words","topics"]}},"required":["message"]}}}},"403":{"description":"The API key lacks the permission this endpoint requires, or the request is outside what the organisation or the API key can access.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"description":"The API key lacks the permission this endpoint requires, or the request is outside what the organisation or the API key can access.","anyOf":[{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The organisation does not own the account, the API key scope does not reach it, or the endpoint is not enabled for the organisation.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"}},"required":["message"]},{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The API key lacks the permission this endpoint requires.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"},"code":{"description":"Machine-readable error code.","type":"string","enum":["API_KEY_PERMISSION_DENIED"]},"details":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"required_permission":{"description":"The permission the API key lacks.","type":"string"}},"required":["required_permission"]}},"required":["message","code","details"]},{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The organization switched on the kill switch for the account's platform, so the API does not serve the account until it is switched off.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"},"code":{"description":"Machine-readable error code.","type":"string","enum":["PLATFORM_KILL_SWITCH_ENABLED"]}},"required":["message","code"]}]}}}}}}}}}
```

## Update the private labels of a fan. (For OnlyFans account only)

> Sets the custom name and the note the account owner keeps for a fan.\
> \
> Both are private to the account owner: they are never shown to the fan and are not the\
> fan's profile name on the platform.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.messages\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"fans","description":"Endpoints for managing fan data of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/fans/{fan_id}":{"patch":{"summary":"Update the private labels of a fan. (For OnlyFans account only)","tags":["fans"],"description":"Sets the custom name and the note the account owner keeps for a fan.\n\nBoth are private to the account owner: they are never shown to the fan and are not the\nfan's profile name on the platform.\n\n**Rate limit:** 1 request per second\n\n**Required permission:** `onlyfans.messages`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"description":"At least one field must be present. Fields left out keep their current value.","anyOf":[{"title":"custom_name provided","additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"custom_name":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"description":"Private label shown to the account owner instead of the fan's nickname. Cannot be empty — there is no way to restore the nickname once a custom name is set.","type":"string"},"note":{"maxLength":200,"description":"Private note about the fan. An empty string clears the note.","type":"string"}},"required":["custom_name"]},{"title":"note provided","additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"custom_name":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"description":"Private label shown to the account owner instead of the fan's nickname. Cannot be empty — there is no way to restore the nickname once a custom name is set.","type":"string"},"note":{"maxLength":200,"description":"Private note about the fan. An empty string clears the note.","type":"string"}},"required":["note"]}]}}},"description":"At least one field must be present. Fields left out keep their current value."},"parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"fan_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default account, the API key scope does not reach it, or the endpoint is not enabled for the organisation.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the requires.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"},"code":{"description":"Machine-readable error lacks.","type":"string"}},"required":["required_permission"]}},"required":["message","code","details"]},{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The organization switched on the kill switch for the account's platform, so the API does not serve the account until it is switched off.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the ## Subscribe the account to a fan. (For OnlyFans account only)

> Makes the account follow the fan on the platform and returns the resulting subscription.\
> Subscribing to a fan the account already follows is not an error: the existing subscription\
> is returned unchanged.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`onlyfans.collections\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"fans","description":"Endpoints for managing fan data of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/fans/{fan_id}/subscribe":{"post":{"summary":"Subscribe the account to a fan. (For OnlyFans account only)","tags":["fans"],"description":"Makes the account follow the fan on the platform and returns the resulting subscription.\nSubscribing to a fan the account already follows is not an error: the existing subscription\nis returned unchanged.\n\n**Rate limit:** 1 request per second\n\n**Required permission:** `onlyfans.collections`","parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"fan_id","required":true,"description":"Big integer id as string"}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"fan":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Identifier of the fan.","type":"string"}},"required":["id"]},"subscribed":{"description":"Whether the account is subscribed to the fan.","type":"boolean"},"subscribed_at":{"description":"Start of the account's subscription to the fan.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"format":"date-time","type":"string"}]},"expires_at":{"description":"End of the account's subscription to the fan. Null when it has no end date.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"format":"date-time","type":"string"}]},"auto_renew_enabled":{"description":"Whether the subscription renews automatically.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"price_gross":{"description":"Price of the subscription, paid by the account, in USD. 0 or null means free.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"number"}]}},"required":["fan","subscribed","subscribed_at","expires_at","auto_renew_enabled","price_gross"]}}}},"400":{"description":"The platform refused the subscription or the request failed validation.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The platform refused the subscription or the request failed validation.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure. ## Create a tracking link on a platform account

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second per endpoint, and 1 request per second per platform account, shared by the endpoints that create and delete tracking and trial links.\
> \
> A \`502\` or \`504\` means whether OnlyFans created the link is unknown: list the links of the account before retrying, or a retry may create a second link.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`traffic\_links.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"statistics","description":"Endpoints for retrieving account statistics."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/platforms/{platform}/accounts/{platform_account_id}/tracking-links":{"post":{"summary":"Create a tracking link on a platform account","tags":["statistics"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second per endpoint, and 1 request per second per platform account, shared by the endpoints that create and delete tracking and trial links.\n\nA `502` or `504` means whether OnlyFans created the link is unknown: list the links of the account before retrying, or a retry may create a second link.\n\n**Required permission:** `traffic_links.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":100,"description":"Link name, unique on the account. OnlyFans checks the uniqueness.","type":"string"}},"required":["name"]}}},"required":true},"parameters":[{"schema":{"type":"string","enum":["onlyfans"]},"in":"path","name":"platform","required":true,"description":"Name of the platform. Currently, only \"onlyfans\" is supported."},{"schema":{"anyOf":[{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Big integer id as string","type":"string"},{"type":"string"}]},"in":"path","name":"platform_account_id","required":true,"description":"Unique account identifier returned by the OnlyFans platform."}],"responses":{"201":{"description":"The created tracking link.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The created tracking link.","type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Link id on OnlyFans.","type":"string"},"name":{"description":"Link name.","type":"string"},"url":{"description":"Link URL.","type":"string"},"created_at":{"format":"date-time","description":"When OnlyFans created the link.","type":"string"}},"required":["id","name","url","created_at"]}}}},"400":{"description":"The body or the path failed validation, OnlyFans rejected the input (for example, a duplicate name or the link limit), or OnlyFans requires face verification on the account (FACE_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED). ## Create a free trial link on a platform account

> OnlyFans allows trial links only on accounts with a paid subscription price.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second per endpoint, and 1 request per second per platform account, shared by the endpoints that create and delete tracking and trial links.\
> \
> A \`502\` or \`504\` means whether OnlyFans created the link is unknown: list the links of the account before retrying, or a retry may create a second link.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`traffic\_links.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"statistics","description":"Endpoints for retrieving account statistics."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/platforms/{platform}/accounts/{platform_account_id}/trial-links":{"post":{"summary":"Create a free trial link on a platform account","tags":["statistics"],"description":"OnlyFans allows trial links only on accounts with a paid subscription price.\n\n**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second per endpoint, and 1 request per second per platform account, shared by the endpoints that create and delete tracking and trial links.\n\nA `502` or `504` means whether OnlyFans created the link is unknown: list the links of the account before retrying, or a retry may create a second link.\n\n**Required permission:** `traffic_links.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"maxLength":64,"description":"Link name. Omitted means the link has no name.","type":"string"},"duration_days":{"description":"Length of the free trial in days.","anyOf":[{"type":"number","enum":[1]},{"type":"number","enum":[3]},{"type":"number","enum":[7]},{"type":"number","enum":[14]},{"type":"number","enum":[30]},{"type":"number","enum":[90]},{"type":"number","enum":[180]},{"type":"number","enum":[360]}]},"expiration_days":{"minimum":0,"maximum":30,"description":"Days until the offer expires; 0 means it never expires.","type":"integer"},"claims_limit":{"description":"How many fans can claim the offer; 0 means no limit.","anyOf":[{"type":"number","enum":[0]},{"type":"number","enum":[1]},{"type":"number","enum":[2]},{"type":"number","enum":[3]},{"type":"number","enum":[4]},{"type":"number","enum":[5]},{"type":"number","enum":[6]},{"type":"number","enum":[7]},{"type":"number","enum":[8]},{"type":"number","enum":[9]},{"type":"number","enum":[10]},{"type":"number","enum":[50]},{"type":"number","enum":[100]}]}},"required":["duration_days","expiration_days","claims_limit"]}}},"required":true},"parameters":[{"schema":{"type":"string","enum":["onlyfans"]},"in":"path","name":"platform","required":true,"description":"Name of the platform. ## Retrieve Accounts Overview metrics for a Fansly account

> The Fansly metrics of the Accounts Overview page for one account and period, with the same values as the page shows.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 15 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`dashboard.view\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"statistics","description":"Endpoints for retrieving account statistics."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/platforms/fansly/accounts/{platform_account_id}/overview-metrics":{"get":{"summary":"Retrieve Accounts Overview metrics for a Fansly account","tags":["statistics"],"description":"The Fansly metrics of the Accounts Overview page for one account and period, with the same values as the page shows.\n\n**Rate limit:** 15 requests per second\n\n**Required permission:** `dashboard.view`","parameters":[{"schema":{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"from","required":true,"description":"Start of the period, ISO 8601 with offset; its calendar date in that offset must not be earlier than 2016-01-01. For the revenue fields, `spenders_count`, `transactions_count` and the averages, a bound that falls inside an hour is moved to the next whole UTC hour: 05:15Z is treated as 06:00Z."},{"schema":{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"to","required":true,"description":"End of the period, ISO 8601 with offset; not earlier than `from`, and its calendar date not more than 365 days after the calendar date of `from`. For the revenue fields, `spenders_count`, `transactions_count` and the averages, a bound that falls inside an hour is moved to the next whole UTC hour: 05:15Z is treated as 06:00Z."},{"schema":{"type":"string","enum":["net","gross"],"default":"net"},"in":"query","name":"earnings_type","required":false,"description":"Whether amounts are net (after the Fansly platform fee) or gross. Applies to every amount in the response."},{"schema":{"type":"boolean"},"in":"query","name":"include_subscription_revenue","required":true,"description":"The Subscription Revenue switch of the Accounts Overview page. When false, subscription earnings and fans who only paid for subscriptions are left out of `total_revenue`, `month_total_revenue`, `spenders_count`, `transactions_count`, `spender_transactions_avg`, `transaction_revenue_avg` and `spender_revenue_avg`, and `subs_revenue` is 0. The fan counts and the tracking-link figures do not depend on it."},{"schema":{"anyOf":[{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Big integer id as string","type":"string"},{"type":"string"}]},"in":"path","name":"platform_account_id","required":true,"description":"Unique account identifier returned by the Fansly platform."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"account_id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"OnlyMonster account identifier.","type":"string"},"platform_account_id":{"description":"Unique account identifier returned by the Fansly platform.","type":"string"},"earnings_type":{"type":"string","enum":["net","gross"],"description":"Whether amounts are net (after the Fansly platform fee) or gross. Applies to every amount in the response."},"monthly_revenue_goal":{"description":"Monthly revenue goal set for the account in OnlyMonster, in US dollars, or null when no goal is set.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"month_total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings from the start of the current calendar month (UTC) to now; independent of `from` and `to`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"subs_revenue":{"description":"Subscription earnings in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"tips_revenue":{"description":"Tip earnings in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"media_revenue":{"description":"Earnings from single media purchases in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"media_sets_revenue":{"description":"Earnings from media set purchases in the period. ## Retrieve Accounts Overview metrics for an OnlyFans account

> The OnlyFans metrics of the Accounts Overview page for one account and period, computed the same way as the page. The API answers for every OnlyFans account the key reaches, also where the page does not show OnlyFans accounts yet.\
> \
> Times are UTC. The period fields count \`from\` and \`to\` as inclusive instants. \`month\_total\_revenue\`, the four \`\*\_run\_rate\` fields, their baselines (\`previous\_month\_total\_revenue\`, \`thirty\_days\_total\_revenue\`, \`previous\_month\_subs\_count\`, \`thirty\_days\_subs\_count\`) and \`monthly\_revenue\_goal\` do not depend on \`from\` and \`to\`: the month and the day windows come from the UTC calendar at request time.\
> \
> To compare two periods, call the endpoint again with the other \`from\` and \`to\` and compare the period fields on your side. The now-anchored fields come back the same in both answers; compare each run rate with its baseline instead: \`monthly\_revenue\_run\_rate\` with \`previous\_month\_total\_revenue\`, \`thirty\_days\_revenue\_run\_rate\` with \`thirty\_days\_total\_revenue\`, \`monthly\_subs\_run\_rate\` with \`previous\_month\_subs\_count\`, \`thirty\_days\_subs\_run\_rate\` with \`thirty\_days\_subs\_count\`.\
> \
> Not returned: the totals row, the KPI tiles, share-of-total percentages, compare-to values and custom metrics.\
> \
> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 15 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`dashboard.view\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"statistics","description":"Endpoints for retrieving account statistics."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/platforms/onlyfans/accounts/{platform_account_id}/overview-metrics":{"get":{"summary":"Retrieve Accounts Overview metrics for an OnlyFans account","tags":["statistics"],"description":"The OnlyFans metrics of the Accounts Overview page for one account and period, computed the same way as the page. The API answers for every OnlyFans account the key reaches, also where the page does not show OnlyFans accounts yet.\n\nTimes are UTC. The period fields count `from` and `to` as inclusive instants. `month_total_revenue`, the four `*_run_rate` fields, their baselines (`previous_month_total_revenue`, `thirty_days_total_revenue`, `previous_month_subs_count`, `thirty_days_subs_count`) and `monthly_revenue_goal` do not depend on `from` and `to`: the month and the day windows come from the UTC calendar at request time.\n\nTo compare two periods, call the endpoint again with the other `from` and `to` and compare the period fields on your side. The now-anchored fields come back the same in both answers; compare each run rate with its baseline instead: `monthly_revenue_run_rate` with `previous_month_total_revenue`, `thirty_days_revenue_run_rate` with `thirty_days_total_revenue`, `monthly_subs_run_rate` with `previous_month_subs_count`, `thirty_days_subs_run_rate` with `thirty_days_subs_count`.\n\nNot returned: the totals row, the KPI tiles, share-of-total percentages, compare-to values and custom metrics.\n\n**Rate limit:** 15 requests per second\n\n**Required permission:** `dashboard.view`","parameters":[{"schema":{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"from","required":true,"description":"Start of the period, ISO 8601 with offset; its calendar date in that offset must not be earlier than 2016-01-01. Inclusive, used as sent: no rounding to the hour."},{"schema":{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"to","required":true,"description":"End of the period, ISO 8601 with offset; not earlier than `from`, and its calendar date not more than 365 days after the calendar date of `from`. Inclusive, used as sent: no rounding to the hour."},{"schema":{"type":"string","enum":["net","gross"],"default":"net"},"in":"query","name":"earnings_type","required":false,"description":"Whether amounts are net (after the OnlyFans platform fee) or gross. Applies to every amount in the response."},{"schema":{"type":"boolean"},"in":"query","name":"include_subscription_revenue","required":true,"description":"The Subscription Revenue switch of the Accounts Overview page. When false, subscription and recurring subscription transactions are left out. `subs_revenue`, `new_paid_fans_subs_revenue` and `renewed_paid_fans_subs_revenue` are 0, and `sales_rate` is always null. These fields get smaller: `total_revenue`, `month_total_revenue`, `spenders_count`, `transactions_count`, `monthly_revenue_run_rate`, `thirty_days_revenue_run_rate`, `previous_month_total_revenue`, `thirty_days_total_revenue`, `new_fans_revenue`, `existing_fans_revenue`, `new_spenders_count`, `new_fan_revenue_avg`, `new_fan_to_spender_conversion_percent`, `tracking_links_revenue` and `trial_links_revenue`. `spender_transactions_avg`, `transaction_revenue_avg`, `spender_revenue_avg` and `like_for_like_revenue` are computed without subscription transactions and can move either way. It does not change `tips_revenue`, `ppv_revenue`, `post_revenue`, `media_revenue`, `sales_revenue`, the fan counts, the subscription run rates, `previous_month_subs_count`, `thirty_days_subs_count` or `monthly_revenue_goal`."},{"schema":{"anyOf":[{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Big integer id as string","type":"string"},{"type":"string"}]},"in":"path","name":"platform_account_id","required":true,"description":"Unique account identifier returned by the OnlyFans platform."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"Default Response","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"account_id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"OnlyMonster account identifier.","type":"string"},"platform_account_id":{"description":"Unique account identifier returned by the OnlyFans platform.","type":"string"},"earnings_type":{"type":"string","enum":["net","gross"],"description":"Whether amounts are net (after the OnlyFans platform fee) or gross. Applies to every amount in the response."},"monthly_revenue_goal":{"description":"Monthly revenue goal set for the account in OnlyMonster, in US dollars, or null when no goal is set.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"month_total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings from the start of the current calendar month (UTC) to now; independent of `from` and `to`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"subs_revenue":{"description":"Subscription earnings (new and recurring subscriptions) in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"tips_revenue":{"description":"Tip earnings in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"media_revenue":{"description":"`ppv_revenue` plus `post_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"ppv_revenue":{"description":"Earnings from paid messages (PPV) in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"post_revenue":{"description":"Earnings from paid posts in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"sales_revenue":{"description":"`ppv_revenue` plus `tips_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"sales_rate":{"description":"`sales_revenue` divided by `subs_revenue`, a ratio that can exceed 1, or null when `subs_revenue` is 0 (always the case with `include_subscription_revenue=false`).","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"spenders_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Fans who paid at least once in the period.","type":"integer"},"transactions_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Paid transactions in the period.","type":"integer"},"spender_transactions_avg":{"description":"Average number of transactions per spender (APC), or null when there are no spenders.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"transaction_revenue_avg":{"description":"Average earnings per transaction (APV), in US dollars, or null when there are no transactions.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"spender_revenue_avg":{"description":"Average earnings per spender (ARPPU), in US dollars, or null when there are no spenders.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"like_for_like_revenue":{"description":"`total_revenue` minus the total earnings of the period of the same length right before `from`. Can be negative. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"monthly_revenue_run_rate":{"description":"Month-to-date earnings divided by the UTC day of the month, times the days in the current UTC month; independent of `from` and `to`. Compare with `previous_month_total_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"thirty_days_revenue_run_rate":{"description":"Earnings of the last 7 full UTC days (ending yesterday) divided by 7, times 30; independent of `from` and `to`. Compare with `thirty_days_total_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"previous_month_total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings over the whole previous UTC calendar month; independent of `from` and `to`. The baseline of `monthly_revenue_run_rate`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"thirty_days_total_revenue":{"description":"Total earnings over the 30 full UTC days ending yesterday; independent of `from` and `to`. The baseline of `thirty_days_revenue_run_rate`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"new_fans_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Fans who subscribed in the period.","type":"integer"},"fans_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Distinct fans with any subscription event in the period.","type":"integer"},"renewed_paid_fans_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Fans with a recurring subscription payment in the period.","type":"integer"},"monthly_subs_run_rate":{"minimum":0,"description":"Month-to-date new subscriptions divided by the UTC day of the month, times the days in the current UTC month, rounded; independent of `from` and `to`. Compare with `previous_month_subs_count`.","type":"integer"},"thirty_days_subs_run_rate":{"minimum":0,"description":"New subscriptions of the last 7 full UTC days (ending yesterday) divided by 7, times 30, rounded; independent of `from` and `to`. Compare with `thirty_days_subs_count`.","type":"integer"},"previous_month_subs_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"New subscriptions over the whole previous UTC calendar month; independent of `from` and `to`. The baseline of `monthly_subs_run_rate`.","type":"integer"},"thirty_days_subs_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"New subscriptions over the 30 full UTC days ending yesterday; independent of `from` and `to`. The baseline of `thirty_days_subs_run_rate`.","type":"integer"},"trial_links_fans_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"Fans who subscribed through a trial link in the period.","type":"integer"},"tracking_links_fans_count":{"description":"Fans who subscribed through a tracking link in the period, or null when this metric is not available for the organization.","anyOf":[{"minimum":0,"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"new_fans_revenue":{"description":"Earnings in the period from fans who subscribed in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"existing_fans_revenue":{"description":"`total_revenue` minus `new_fans_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"new_spenders_count":{"minimum":0,"description":"New fans of the period who also paid in it.","type":"integer"},"new_paid_fans_subs_revenue":{"description":"Subscription earnings from fans who subscribed in the period. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"renewed_paid_fans_subs_revenue":{"description":"`subs_revenue` minus `new_paid_fans_subs_revenue`. In US dollars.","type":"number"},"new_fan_revenue_avg":{"description":"`new_fans_revenue` divided by `new_fans_count`, in US dollars, or null when there are no new fans in the period.","anyOf":[{"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"new_fan_to_spender_conversion_percent":{"description":"Share of new fans who also paid in the period, 0–100 (`new_spenders_count` divided by `new_fans_count`, times 100), or null when there are no new fans in the period.","anyOf":[{"minimum":0,"maximum":100,"type":"number"},{"type":"null"}]},"tracking_links_revenue":{"description":"Earnings in the period from fans who came through tracking links. ## Retrieve user metrics for OnlyFans platform

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 15 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`team\_metrics.view\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"statistics","description":"Endpoints for retrieving account statistics."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/users/metrics":{"get":{"summary":"Retrieve user metrics for OnlyFans platform","description":"**Rate limit:** 15 requests per second\n\n**Required permission:** `team_metrics.view`","tags":["statistics"],"parameters":[{"name":"from","in":"query","required":true,"schema":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},"description":"Start of the metrics timestamp range (ISO 8601 Zulu)."},{"name":"to","in":"query","required":true,"schema":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},"description":"End of the metrics timestamp range (ISO 8601 Zulu)."},{"name":"creator_ids","in":"query","required":false,"schema":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"integer","minimum":1},"minItems":1,"maxItems":100},"description":"Array of creator IDs to filter metrics by."},{"name":"user_ids","in":"query","required":false,"schema":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"integer","minimum":1},"minItems":1,"maxItems":100},"description":"Array of user IDs to filter metrics by."},{"name":"account_group_id","in":"query","required":false,"schema":{"type":"integer"},"description":"Account group ID to filter metrics by."},{"name":"role_id","in":"query","required":false,"schema":{"type":"integer"},"description":"Role ID to filter metrics by."},{"name":"offset","in":"query","required":true,"schema":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"default":0},"description":"Offset for 100)."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"User metrics successfully AI replies, copy-pasted messages, OnlyFans templates and internal templates are excluded"},"typed_messages_count":{"type":"integer","description":"Number of manually typed messages, same exclusions as words_count_sum; the denominator for the words average"},"unsent_messages_count":{"type":"integer","description":"Number of unsent interval"},"purchase_interval_max":{"type":"integer","description":"Maximum purchase interval"},"sold_posts_count":{"type":"integer","description":"Number of posts ## Export an uploaded media to the vault.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 3 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Account limit:\*\* 3 requests per minute per platform account, shared by \`uploads/export\` and \`uploads/{upload\_id}/retry\` — the pace OnlyFans accepts exports for one account. Over it the request answers \`429\` with a \`Retry-After\` header.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`media\_uploader.manage\`

## Retry a failed vault media upload.

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 3 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Account limit:\*\* 3 requests per minute per platform account, shared by \`uploads/export\` and \`uploads/{upload\_id}/retry\` — the pace OnlyFans accepts exports for one account. Over it the request answers \`429\` with a \`Retry-After\` header.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`media\_uploader.manage\`

```json

Null when the campaign never recalls.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"unsend_if_not_read_enabled":{"description":"Whether the recall is skipped for a message the fan has already opened. False means the message is recalled whatever the fan did.","type":"boolean"},"unsend_if_not_replied_enabled":{"description":"Whether the recall is skipped for a message the fan replied to. A fan who opened the message without replying keeps it too, the same as with `unsend_if_not_read_enabled`.","type":"boolean"},"retry_after_unsend_min":{"description":"Minutes after a recall before the same fan may be targeted again. Null when a fan is targeted at most once, which is the default.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"end_at":{"description":"When the campaign stops firing (ISO 8601 Zulu). Null when it runs until stopped by hand.","anyOf":[{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},{"type":"null"}]},"mimic_on_reply_enabled":{"description":"Whether a reply from the fan opens a Mimic chat.","type":"boolean"}},"required":["send_delay_interval","send_after_expire_min","send_before_expire_min","unsend_delay_sec","unsend_if_not_read_enabled","unsend_if_not_replied_enabled","retry_after_unsend_min","end_at","mimic_on_reply_enabled"]},"audience_filters":{"description":"Filters evaluated per fan when the trigger fires. Null for a message that carries only media.","anyOf":[{"type":"string"},{"type":"null"}]},"price_gross":{"minimum":0,"description":"Price the fan pays for this message, before any platform deduction. 0 when the message is free.","type":"number"},"delay_sec":{"description":"Delay after the previous message of the campaign before this one is sent. Null for the first message, which is timed by the campaign rules instead.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"attachments":{"description":"Media attached to this message. Empty when it carries none.","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Identifier of the media on the platform.","type":"string"},"type":{"type":"string","enum":["image","video","audio","gif"]},"is_paid":{"description":"True when the attachment sits behind the paywall and is hidden from the preview the fan sees before paying.","type":"boolean"}},"required":["id","type","is_paid"]}},"text_is_paid":{"description":"True when the text itself sits behind the paywall, so the fan buys the message to read it rather than only to open its media. False when the text is visible before paying, including when the message carries no price at all.","type":"boolean"},"tagged_platform_account_ids":{"description":"Platform accounts tagged in this message, by their identifier on the platform. Empty when it tags none.","type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}},"required":["step","text","price_gross","delay_sec","attachments","text_is_paid","tagged_platform_account_ids"]}},"targeted_sends_count":{"description":"Dispatches the campaign has made since it was created. Cumulative: a fan targeted twice counts twice, and the figure never decreases.","type":"integer"},"first_message_stats":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"Figures for the first message of the campaign only. Follow-up steps are reported separately.","type":"object","properties":{"sent_messages_count":{"description":"Messages recorded as sent. ## Create an Auto Messages campaign

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> The campaign starts running as soon as it is created: it begins matching fans the moment this request is answered. There is no way to stage one — use the status endpoint to pause it.\
> \
> An empty \`audience\_filters\` targets every fan that fires the trigger. Campaigns here are trigger-driven and send one message per fan event, so this is the ordinary shape of a welcome campaign rather than a broadcast.\
> \
> \`audience\_filters\[].options.lists\[].name\` is accepted and never published back: a name is required for each fan list, while the read endpoints carry identifiers alone.\
> \
> \*\*This endpoint is not idempotent.\*\* A request that times out may still have created a running campaign. Before retrying, list the account campaigns and look for one already there; a duplicate can be stopped with the status endpoint. Listing the campaigns needs \`auto\_messages.view\` in addition to \`auto\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`auto\_messages.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"auto-message-campaigns","description":"Endpoints for reading the Auto Messages campaigns of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/auto-message-campaigns":{"post":{"summary":"Create an Auto Messages campaign","tags":["auto-message-campaigns"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\nThe campaign starts running as soon as it is created: it begins matching fans the moment this request is answered. There is no way to stage one — use the status endpoint to pause it.\n\nAn empty `audience_filters` targets every fan that fires the trigger. Campaigns here are trigger-driven and send one message per fan event, so this is the ordinary shape of a welcome campaign rather than a broadcast.\n\n`audience_filters[].options.lists[].name` is accepted and never published back: a name is required for each fan list, while the read endpoints carry identifiers alone.\n\n**This endpoint is not idempotent.** A request that times out may still have created a running campaign. Before retrying, list the account campaigns and look for one already there; a duplicate can be stopped with the status endpoint. Listing the campaigns needs `auto_messages.view` in addition to `auto_messages.edit`.\n\n**Required permission:** `auto_messages.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"trigger":{"type":"string","enum":["welcome_message","online_message","has_expired_message","will_expire_in_message"]},"action":{"type":"string","enum":["send_message","start_mimic"]},"name":{"description":"Campaign name. Required for a sequence and for a message that carries media but no text.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"minLength":1,"maxLength":512,"pattern":"\\S","type":"string"}]},"rules":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"send_delay_interval":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"min_sec":{"enum":[60,180,300,900,1800],"description":"How long to wait before sending. One of the published delays; each one fixes its own `range_sec`.","type":"integer"},"range_sec":{"enum":[120,600,900,1800],"description":"The spread added to the delay. ## Retrieve one Auto Messages campaign

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`auto\_messages.view\`

## Start, pause or archive an Auto Messages campaign

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> Asking for the state the campaign is already in is accepted and does not change the campaign, but it is not free: the change is carried out again, which is how a change that did not complete is retried. If the answer is \`502\`, repeat the same request to complete the change; after a \`204\` there is nothing to repeat. Every request, a repeat included, counts toward the rate limit.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`auto\_messages.edit\`

```json

If the answer is `502`, repeat the same request to complete the change; after a `204` there is nothing to repeat. Every request, a repeat included, counts toward the rate limit.\n\n**Required permission:** `auto_messages.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"State to move the campaign to.\n`active` — the campaign reacts to its trigger cleared.","type":"object","properties":{"status":{"type":"string","enum":["active","paused","archived"]}},"required":["status"]}}},"required":true,"description":"State to move the campaign Repeating the request is safe and is how the change is retried.\nOn a stop the campaign already reads as stopped and starts no new sends; messages already on their way may still be delivered.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"},"code":{"description":"Machine-readable error code.","type":"string","enum":["CAMPAIGN_STATUS_NOT_CONFIRMED"]}},"required":["message"]}}}}}}}}}

## Recall what an Auto Messages campaign has sent

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> The campaign stops sending, its queue is cleared, and the messages it delivered within the last 24 hours are taken back out of the fans' chats. Anything older stays: the platform allows no recall beyond that window.\
> \
> \*\*This cannot be undone and it is not limited to campaigns this API created.\*\* Any campaign of the account can be recalled, including one a member built in the panel.\
> \
> Unlike a status change, repeating this request is not always safe: a campaign that is already recalling refuses it. If the answer is \`502\`, read the campaign before repeating — recalling or recalled means the recall started and finishes on its own, while active or paused means it never started and only a repeat performs it. Reading the campaign needs \`auto\_messages.view\` in addition to \`auto\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`auto\_messages.edit\`

```json

If the answer is `502`, read the campaign before repeating — recalling or recalled means the recall started and finishes on its own, while active or paused means it never started and only a repeat performs it. Reading the campaign needs `auto_messages.view` in addition to `auto_messages.edit`.\n\n**Required permission:** `auto_messages.edit`","parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account the campaign belongs to."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"campaign_id","required":true,"description":"Campaign identifier."}],"responses":{"204":{"description":"The recall was accepted: the campaign stops sending and takes back what it can."},"400":{"description":"The campaign is not in a state a recall can start from: it is already recalling, or it is archived, or it opens a Mimic chat and sends nothing of its own. One code covers every cause; read the campaign to tell which.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The campaign is not in a state a recall can start from: it is already recalling, or it is archived, or it opens a Mimic chat and sends nothing of its own. blindly: the campaign stops sending the moment the recall is accepted, before the recall itself is confirmed, so a repeat against a campaign that did start meets the recall branch and is refused.\nRead the campaign to tell the two apart. Recalling or recalled means it started, and it is finished without further requests. Active or paused means the request never reached the campaign, and repeating it is the only way the recall happens.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"Unlike a status change, this answer is not an invitation to repeat the request blindly: the campaign stops sending the moment the recall is accepted, before the recall itself is confirmed, so a repeat against a campaign that did start meets the recall branch and is refused.\nRead the campaign to tell the two apart. ## Retrieve the Priority Mass Messages broadcasts of an account

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> A schedule reads back as \`null\` once the broadcast has been dispatched; a broadcast paused before its schedule came due keeps it.\
> \
> Sent, unsent and purchased figures, and the purchased amount, count the first message of the broadcast only; replies are counted across every step. Because of that a sequence can report more replies than sends, and its \`reply\_rate\` then reads \`null\` rather than a figure above 1.\
> \
> A broadcast whose content could not be read is listed with \`messages: \[]\` and every figure at zero, rather than failing the page.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`priority\_mass\_messages.view\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"priority-mass-message-campaigns","description":"Endpoints for reading, creating, pausing and recalling the Priority Mass Messages broadcasts of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/priority-mass-message-campaigns":{"get":{"summary":"Retrieve the Priority Mass Messages broadcasts of an account","tags":["priority-mass-message-campaigns"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\nA schedule reads back as `null` once the broadcast has been dispatched; a broadcast paused before its schedule came due keeps it.\n\nSent, unsent and purchased figures, and the purchased amount, count the first message of the broadcast only; replies are counted across every step. Because of that a sequence can report more replies than sends, and its `reply_rate` then reads `null` rather than a figure above 1.\n\nA broadcast whose content could not be read is listed with `messages: []` and every figure at zero, rather than failing the page.\n\n**Required permission:** `priority_mass_messages.view`","parameters":[{"schema":{"minimum":1,"maximum":100,"default":50,"type":"integer"},"in":"query","name":"limit","required":false,"description":"Items per page (min: 1, max: 100, default: 50)."},{"schema":{"minimum":0,"default":0,"type":"integer"},"in":"query","name":"offset","required":false,"description":"Items to skip (default: 0)."},{"schema":{"default":false,"type":"boolean"},"in":"query","name":"include_archived","required":false,"description":"Include archived broadcasts in the page (default: false). Only `archived` is affected — every other state is always listed."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account whose broadcasts are read."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"A page of the account Priority Mass Messages broadcasts, newest first.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"A page of the account Priority Mass Messages broadcasts, newest first.","type":"object","properties":{"items":{"type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Broadcast identifier.","type":"string"},"account_id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"OnlyMonster account the broadcast belongs to.","type":"string"},"name":{"description":"Broadcast name as the account owner set it. Null for a broadcast created without a name whose content could not be read.","anyOf":[{"type":"string"},{"type":"null"}]},"status":{"description":"State of the broadcast, as the panel shows it. Null for a broadcast holding a state this API does not name, which no broadcast created through it can reach.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string","enum":["scheduled","queued","sending","paused","unsending","sent","unsent","archived"]}]},"pause_reason":{"description":"Why the broadcast is paused: `manual` when a person paused it, `send_errors` when too many sends failed, `recipient_import_failed` when the recipients of the broadcast could not be loaded. Null when it is not paused.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string","enum":["manual","send_errors","recipient_import_failed"]}]},"progress_percent":{"minimum":0,"maximum":100,"description":"How far the broadcast has got through its audience, from 0 to 100.","type":"integer"},"created_by":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"Who created the campaign.","type":"object","properties":{"type":{"type":"string","enum":["user","api_key"]},"id":{"description":"For `user`, the id of the member who created the campaign in OnlyMonster; for `api_key`, the id of the API key that created it. Null when the creator is unknown.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]}},"required":["type","id"]},"created_at":{"format":"date-time","description":"When the broadcast was created (ISO 8601 Zulu).","type":"string"},"scheduled_at":{"description":"When the broadcast is due to go out (ISO 8601 Zulu). Null once it has been dispatched, and null for one that was never scheduled; a broadcast paused before its schedule came due keeps it.","anyOf":[{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},{"type":"null"}]},"scheduled_at_timezone":{"description":"IANA zone name the schedule was stated in, so a client can show it the way the panel does. Null whenever `scheduled_at` is null.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]},"rules":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"Who the broadcast sends to, and on what terms it recalls what it sent.","type":"object","properties":{"online_check_mode":{"description":"Whether the broadcast waits for the fan to be online before sending, and on what terms. Null when the broadcast never recalls.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"unsend_if_not_read_enabled":{"description":"Whether the recall is skipped for a message the fan has already opened. ```

## Create a Priority Mass Messages broadcast

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 1 request per second\
> \
> \*\*A created broadcast is a real broadcast to its whole audience, not a draft.\*\* Without a schedule it is queued and goes out without further action; with one it waits for the hour it names. Use the status endpoint to pause it.\
> \
> \*\*This endpoint is not idempotent, and a repeated create is a second broadcast to every fan it matches.\*\* A request that times out, or that answers \`500\` or \`502\`, may still have created one: whether it exists is unknown, so list the account broadcasts before retrying and pause a duplicate at once. Listing the broadcasts needs \`priority\_mass\_messages.view\` in addition to \`priority\_mass\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> A schedule and an end are each a wall-clock date and whole hour plus the clock it is read on. An offset carried by the value is ignored. A schedule in the past goes out at once, an end before the schedule is accepted, and a broadcast whose end passes completes as sent whatever state it was in.\
> \
> A broadcast reads its schedule back as the UTC instant plus the zone name, not as the wall clock it was stated on, and reads it back as \`null\` once it has been dispatched.\
> \
> \`audience\_filters\[].options.lists\[].name\` is required on every fan list and never published back: the reads carry identifiers alone.\
> \
> Revoking or narrowing the key that created a broadcast does not stop it. Pause it through the status endpoint instead.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`priority\_mass\_messages.edit\`

```json

## Retrieve one Priority Mass Messages broadcast

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> A schedule reads back as \`null\` once the broadcast has been dispatched; a broadcast paused before its schedule came due keeps it.\
> \
> Sent, unsent and purchased figures, and the purchased amount, count the first message of the broadcast only; replies are counted across every step. Because of that a sequence can report more replies than sends, and its \`reply\_rate\` then reads \`null\` rather than a figure above 1.\
> \
> A broadcast whose content could not be read is refused with \`502\` here, because that content is exactly what was asked for.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`priority\_mass\_messages.view\`

```json

Because of that a sequence can report more replies than sends, and its `reply_rate` then reads `null` rather than a figure above 1.\n\nA broadcast whose content could not be read is refused with `502` here, because that content is exactly what was asked for.\n\n**Required permission:** `priority_mass_messages.view`","parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account the broadcast belongs to."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"campaign_id","required":true,"description":"Broadcast identifier."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"One Priority Mass Messages broadcast of the account.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"One Priority Mass Messages broadcast of the account.","type":"object","properties":{"id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Broadcast identifier.","type":"string"},"account_id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"OnlyMonster account the broadcast belongs to.","type":"string"},"name":{"description":"Broadcast name as the account owner set it. ## Pause or resume a Priority Mass Messages broadcast

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> The answer says which state the broadcast holds now, which is not always the one asked for: resuming a broadcast paused before its schedule came due answers \`scheduled\`, because it keeps that schedule.\
> \
> A paused broadcast can be resumed for five days; after a further period it is archived automatically and can no longer be resumed. There is no manual archive.\
> \
> Asking for a state the broadcast already holds is accepted and changes nothing. A change that fails for a reason that is not about the request answers a plain \`502\` with no code.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`priority\_mass\_messages.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"priority-mass-message-campaigns","description":"Endpoints for reading, creating, pausing and recalling the Priority Mass Messages broadcasts of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/priority-mass-message-campaigns/{campaign_id}/status":{"patch":{"summary":"Pause or resume a Priority Mass Messages broadcast","tags":["priority-mass-message-campaigns"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\nThe answer says which state the broadcast holds now, which is not always the one asked for: resuming a broadcast paused before its schedule came due answers `scheduled`, because it keeps that schedule.\n\nA paused broadcast can be resumed for five days; after a further period it is archived automatically and can no longer be resumed. There is no manual archive.\n\nAsking for a state the broadcast already holds is accepted and changes nothing. A change that fails for a reason that is not about the request answers a plain `502` with no code.\n\n**Required permission:** `priority_mass_messages.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"State to move the broadcast to.\n`paused` — the broadcast stops sending. Accepted from scheduled, queued and sending; pausing a paused broadcast changes nothing.\n`queued` — the broadcast resumes. Accepted for a paused broadcast within the resume window, and for one already queued, which changes nothing.","type":"object","properties":{"status":{"type":"string","enum":["paused","queued"]}},"required":["status"]}}},"required":true,"description":"State to move the broadcast to.\n`paused` — the broadcast stops sending.
