## Recall what a Priority Mass Messages broadcast sent

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> The broadcast stops sending and the messages it delivered are taken back out of the fans' chats, as far as the platform's 24-hour recall window allows. A recall is possible within 24 hours of the broadcast's last status change.\
> \
> \*\*This cannot be undone and it is not limited to broadcasts this API created.\*\* Any broadcast of the account can be recalled, including one a person built in the panel.\
> \
> A \`502\` does not promise the broadcast is untouched, but no repeat recalls twice, so repeating the request is safe; read the broadcast if the answer stays \`502\`. This differs from the Auto Messages recall, whose \`502\` asks the caller to read the campaign before repeating at all. Reading the broadcast needs \`priority\_mass\_messages.view\` in addition to \`priority\_mass\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`priority\_mass\_messages.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"priority-mass-message-campaigns","description":"Endpoints for reading, creating, pausing and recalling the Priority Mass Messages broadcasts of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/priority-mass-message-campaigns/{campaign_id}/unsend":{"post":{"summary":"Recall what a Priority Mass Messages broadcast sent","tags":["priority-mass-message-campaigns"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\nThe broadcast stops sending and the messages it delivered are taken back out of the fans' chats, as far as the platform's 24-hour recall window allows. A recall is possible within 24 hours of the broadcast's last status change.\n\n**This cannot be undone and it is not limited to broadcasts this API created.** Any broadcast of the account can be recalled, including one a person built in the panel.\n\nA `502` does not promise the broadcast is untouched, but no repeat recalls twice, so repeating the request is safe; read the broadcast if the answer stays `502`. This differs from the Auto Messages recall, whose `502` asks the caller to read the campaign before repeating at all. Reading the broadcast needs `priority_mass_messages.view` in addition to `priority_mass_messages.edit`.\n\n**Required permission:** `priority_mass_messages.edit`","parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account the broadcast belongs to."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"campaign_id","required":true,"description":"Broadcast identifier."}],"responses":{"204":{"description":"The recall was applied: the broadcast stops sending and takes back what it can."},"400":{"description":"The broadcast is not in a state a recall can start from: the wrong state, a paused broadcast that never started sending, or one whose last status change is more than 24 hours old. One code covers every cause; read the broadcast to tell which.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The broadcast is not in a state a recall can start from: the wrong state, a paused broadcast that never started sending, or one whose last status change is more than 24 hours old. One code covers every cause; read the broadcast to tell which.","type":"object","properties":{"message":{"description":"Human-readable explanation of the failure.","type":"string"},"code":{"description":"Machine-readable error code.","type":"string","enum":["CAMPAIGN_NOT_RECALLABLE"]}},"required":["message"]}}}},"401":{"description":"The token is missing or invalid.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The token is missing or ## Take a Mass Message back

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*This cannot be undone and it reaches every Mass Message of the account, not only one this API sent\*\* — including one a person sent from the panel.\
> \
> OnlyFans decides how long a Mass Message can be taken back for; once that window closes the request is refused. A \`scheduled\` Mass Message is cancelled before it goes out.\
> \
> Listing or reading a Mass Message needs \`mass\_messages.view\` in addition to \`mass\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`mass\_messages.edit\`

```json

A `scheduled` Mass Message is cancelled before it goes out.\n\nListing or reading a Mass Message needs `mass_messages.view` in addition to `mass_messages.edit`.\n\n**Required permission:** ## Send a Mass Message from an account

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> \*\*A send is a real broadcast to every fan of the selected lists, not a draft, and this endpoint is not idempotent.\*\* A send without \`scheduled\_at\` is never refused as a duplicate: sending it again, a retry after a timeout or a \`502\` included, is a second Mass Message to every one of those fans. Only a send with \`scheduled\_at\` repeated within 45 s is refused with a \`409\`; after that, it is a second scheduled Mass Message.\
> \
> With \`scheduled\_at\` the platform holds the Mass Message and sends it at that moment. Until then it lists as \`scheduled\`; the unsend endpoint cancels it.\
> \
> A \`502\` means whether the Mass Message went out is unknown: list the Mass Messages of the account before retrying, and unsend a duplicate while the platform window allows it.\
> \
> Listing or reading a Mass Message needs \`mass\_messages.view\` in addition to \`mass\_messages.edit\`.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`mass\_messages.edit\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"mass-messages","description":"Endpoints for sending, listing, reading and unsending the Mass Messages of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/mass-messages":{"post":{"summary":"Send a Mass Message from an account","tags":["mass-messages"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\n**A send is a real broadcast to every fan of the selected lists, not a draft, and this endpoint is not idempotent.** A send without `scheduled_at` is never refused as a duplicate: sending it again, a retry after a timeout or a `502` included, is a second Mass Message to every one of those fans. Only a send with `scheduled_at` repeated within 45 s is refused with a `409`; after that, it is a second scheduled Mass Message.\n\nWith `scheduled_at` the platform holds the Mass Message and sends it at that moment. Until then it lists as `scheduled`; the unsend endpoint cancels it.\n\nA `502` means whether the Mass Message went out is unknown: list the Mass Messages of the account before retrying, and unsend a duplicate while the platform window allows it.\n\nListing or reading a Mass Message needs `mass_messages.view` in addition to `mass_messages.edit`.\n\n**Required permission:** `mass_messages.edit`","requestBody":{"content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"text":{"description":"Text the fan receives. Null for a message that carries only media or a GIF. A message carries text, at least one attachment, or a GIF.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"maxLength":5000,"type":"string"}]},"text_is_paid":{"description":"True to put the text itself behind the paywall, so the fan buys the message to read it. Requires text that is not empty or whitespace.","type":"boolean"},"price_gross":{"description":"Price the fan pays for this message, before any platform deduction. 0 makes it free; any other amount is between 3 and 200. A price requires something behind the paywall — `text_is_paid` or an attachment with `is_paid` — and either of those requires a price.","anyOf":[{"type":"number","enum":[0]},{"minimum":3,"maximum":200,"type":"number"}]},"attachments":{"maxItems":100,"description":"Media to attach. Empty for a message that carries none. A GIF is not an attachment — it travels as `giphy_id`.","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Identifier of the media on the platform.","type":"string"},"type":{"type":"string","enum":["image","video","audio"],"description":"Kind of media being attached."},"is_paid":{"description":"True to put the attachment behind the paywall, hiding it from the preview the fan sees before paying.","type":"boolean"}},"required":["id","type","is_paid"]}},"giphy_id":{"description":"Identifier of the GIF to send, or null to send none.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"maxLength":128,"type":"string"}]},"user_list_ids":{"maxItems":100,"description":"Fan lists this message is sent to, up to 100. Empty to send to every fan except the excluded lists; at least one list is stated here or in `excluded_user_list_ids`.","type":"array","items":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":64,"description":"Identifier of a fan list on the platform.","type":"string"}},"excluded_user_list_ids":{"maxItems":100,"description":"Fan lists to leave out of the audience. Empty to leave out none.","type":"array","items":{"minLength":1,"maxLength":64,"description":"Identifier of a fan list on the platform.","type":"string"}},"tagged_platform_account_ids":{"maxItems":10,"description":"Platform accounts to tag in this message, by their identifier on the platform.","type":"array","items":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"description":"Big integer id as string","type":"string"}},"scheduled_at":{"format":"date-time","description":"When the platform should send the Mass Message, as an ISO 8601 date-time with an offset (`Z` for UTC). The value is read as an instant and stored in UTC. Seconds and milliseconds are dropped, since the platform keeps the minute, and the response carries that minute. It must be at least one minute and at most one year ahead, counted from that minute. Omit to send right away. Refused with 400 while scheduling is not enabled for the organisation. A scheduled Mass Message lists as `scheduled` until then and is cancelled with the unsend endpoint.","type":"string"}},"required":["text","text_is_paid","price_gross","attachments","giphy_id","user_list_ids","excluded_user_list_ids","tagged_platform_account_ids"]}}},"required":true},"parameters":[{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account whose Mass Messages are addressed."}],"responses":{"201":{"description":"The queue state of the Mass Message that was just sent.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The queue state of the Mass Message that was just sent.","type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Identifier of the Mass Message.","type":"string"},"status":{"type":"string","enum":["scheduled","sending","done","unsent"],"description":"Where the Mass Message stands: `scheduled` while it waits for `scheduled_at`, `sending` while the platform is still delivering it, `done` once every message has gone out, `unsent` once it has been taken back. A `scheduled` Mass Message is cancelled with the unsend endpoint."},"created_at":{"format":"date-time","description":"When the Mass Message was queued.","type":"string"},"targeted_recipients_count":{"description":"Fans the Mass Message is going out to. Null while the platform is still counting the audience.","anyOf":[{"type":"integer"},{"type":"null"}]},"pending_messages_count":{"description":"Messages of this Mass Message still waiting to be sent.","type":"integer"},"can_unsend":{"description":"True while the Mass Message can still be taken back.","type":"boolean"},"unsend_window_sec":{"description":"Seconds the platform allows a Mass Message to be taken back within.","type":"integer"},"scheduled_at":{"description":"When the platform sends the Mass Message, in UTC. ## Retrieve the Mass Messages of an account

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> Pagination is by cursor: set \`to\` to the \`created\_at\` of the last item of the page just read. The cursor has one-second precision — deduplicate by \`id\`.\
> \
> The first page starts with every scheduled Mass Message, soonest first, then every Mass Message still sending, then the history newest first. The scheduled and sending ones are not counted against \`limit\`, so the first page may hold more than \`limit\` items. It is the only page that carries \`total\`; \`total\` reads \`null\` on every page after it.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`mass\_messages.view\`

```json
{"openapi":"3.0.0","info":{"title":"om-api-service","version":"0.77.0"},"tags":[{"name":"mass-messages","description":"Endpoints for sending, listing, reading and unsending the Mass Messages of an account."}],"servers":[{"url":"https://omapi.onlymonster.ai","description":"Production"}],"security":[{"ApiToken":[]}],"components":{"securitySchemes":{"ApiToken":{"type":"apiKey","in":"header","name":"x-om-auth-token","description":"API token for authentication"}}},"paths":{"/api/v0/accounts/{account_id}/mass-messages":{"get":{"summary":"Retrieve the Mass Messages of an account","tags":["mass-messages"],"description":"**Rate limit:** 5 requests per second\n\nPagination is by cursor: set `to` to the `created_at` of the last item of the page just read. The cursor has one-second precision — deduplicate by `id`.\n\nThe first page starts with every scheduled Mass Message, soonest first, then every Mass Message still sending, then the history newest first. The scheduled and sending ones are not counted against `limit`, so the first page may hold more than `limit` items. It is the only page that carries `total`; `total` reads `null` on every page after it.\n\n**Required permission:** `mass_messages.view`","parameters":[{"schema":{"minimum":1,"maximum":100,"default":50,"type":"integer"},"in":"query","name":"limit","required":false,"description":"Items per page (min: 1, max: 100, default: 50)."},{"schema":{"format":"date-time","type":"string"},"in":"query","name":"to","required":false,"description":"Cursor: the `created_at` of the last item of the previous page."},{"schema":{"pattern":"^(0|[1-9]\\d*)$","maxLength":19,"type":"string"},"in":"path","name":"account_id","required":true,"description":"OnlyMonster account whose Mass Messages are addressed."}],"responses":{"200":{"description":"A page of the account's Mass Messages. The first page starts with every scheduled message, soonest first, then every message still sending, then the history newest first; the scheduled and sending ones are not counted against `limit`, so it may hold more than `limit` items.","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"A page of the account's Mass Messages. The first page starts with every scheduled message, soonest first, then every message still sending, then the history newest first; the scheduled and sending ones are not counted against `limit`, so it may hold more than `limit` items.","type":"object","properties":{"items":{"type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Identifier of the Mass Message.","type":"string"},"status":{"type":"string","enum":["scheduled","sending","done","unsent"],"description":"Where the Mass Message stands: `scheduled` while it waits for `scheduled_at`, `sending` while the platform is still delivering it, `done` once every message has gone out, `unsent` once it has been taken back. A `scheduled` Mass Message is cancelled with the unsend endpoint."},"can_unsend":{"description":"True while the Mass Message can still be taken back.","type":"boolean"},"created_at":{"format":"date-time","description":"When the Mass Message was queued.","type":"string"},"created_by":{"description":"Who sent the Mass Message: `user` with the identifier of the member, `api_key` with the identifier of the API key, resolvable through `GET /api/v0/api-keys`. `null` when the author is unknown.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"type":{"type":"string","enum":["user","api_key"]},"id":{"type":"string"}},"required":["type","id"]}]},"scheduled_at":{"description":"When the platform sends the Mass Message, in UTC. Null for a Mass Message sent right away.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"format":"date-time","type":"string"}]},"message":{"additionalProperties":false,"description":"The content of a Mass Message.","type":"object","properties":{"text":{"description":"Text the fan receives. Null when the message carries none.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]},"text_is_paid":{"description":"Whether the text sits behind the paywall. Null when it is not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]},"price_gross":{"description":"Price the fan pays for this message, before any platform deduction. Null when it is not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"number"}]},"attachments":{"description":"Media attached to the message. Empty when it carries none.","type":"array","items":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"description":"Identifier of the media on the platform.","type":"string"},"type":{"type":"string","enum":["image","video","audio"],"description":"Kind of media attached."},"is_paid":{"description":"Whether the attachment sits behind the paywall. Null when the paid/free split is not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"boolean"}]}},"required":["id","type","is_paid"]}},"giphy_id":{"description":"Identifier of the GIF sent with the message. Null when it carries none.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]},"user_list_ids":{"description":"Fan lists the message was sent to, as identifiers. Null when they are not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}]},"excluded_user_list_ids":{"description":"Fan lists left out of the audience, as identifiers. Null when they are not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}]},"tagged_platform_account_ids":{"description":"Platform accounts tagged in the message. Null when they are not known for this message.","anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}]}},"required":["text","text_is_paid","price_gross","attachments","giphy_id","user_list_ids","excluded_user_list_ids","tagged_platform_account_ids"]},"stats":{"additionalProperties":false,"type":"object","properties":{"sent_messages_count":{"description":"Messages the platform has sent.","type":"integer"},"viewed_messages_count":{"description":"Messages a fan has opened.","type":"integer"},"purchased_messages_count":{"description":"Messages a fan has paid for.","type":"integer"},"purchased_amount_gross":{"description":"Price times purchases, before any platform deduction.","type":"number"}},"required":["sent_messages_count","viewed_messages_count","purchased_messages_count","purchased_amount_gross"]}},"required":["id","status","can_unsend","created_at","created_by","scheduled_at","message","stats"]}},"has_more":{"description":"True when more Mass Messages exist beyond this page.","type":"boolean"},"total":{"description":"The account's number of Mass Messages. ## Retrieve one Mass Message of an account

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 5 requests per second\
> \
> The Mass Message with its fan lists and paid split. It carries no \`status\`, no \`stats\`, no \`created\_at\` and no \`created\_by\` — read those on the list.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`mass\_messages.view\`

```json

## List the API keys of the organisation

> \*\*Rate limit:\*\* 15 requests per second\
> \
> Lists every API key of the organisation of the requesting API key. The key value, its permissions and its scopes are never returned.\
> \
> Deleted keys are listed too, with \`deleted\_at\` set. Keys are hard-deleted after the retention window, so an old \`created\_by.id\` may no longer resolve to a key in this list.\
> \
> An API key restricted to some accounts still lists every key of its organisation.\
> \
> \*\*Required permission:\*\* \`api\_keys.view\`

```json
